- **workflow-manual**: Run the claude-runway ticket workflow end to end (blind plans and plan, GitHub issue, worktree branch, implement, test, commit, one review round of code-review, Codex and Copilot, PR, PR review loop, merge). Asks only for the planning mode, to approve the plan and issue, before merging the PR into main, and before publishing a version's GitHub release; everything in between runs without asking unless something needs a decision. Use to start new work or resume a ticket in claude-runway. ([tmathura/claude-runway](https://github.com/tmathura/claude-runway/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/workflow-manual))
- **workflow-manual**: Run the copilot-plugin-cc ticket workflow end to end (blind plans and plan, GitHub issue, worktree branch, implement, test, commit, one review round of code-review, Codex and Copilot, PR, PR review loop, merge). Asks only for the planning mode, to approve the plan and issue, before merging the PR into main, and before publishing a version's GitHub release; everything in between runs without asking unless something needs a decision. Use to start new work or resume a ticket in copilot-plugin-cc. ([tmathura/copilot-plugin-cc](https://github.com/tmathura/copilot-plugin-cc/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/workflow-manual))
- **workflow-map**: Use when a student wants to make, start, update, or record a run on a map of their research workflow: from a photo or drawing of their research process, from Claude's reading of that drawing, from a workflow.md, or from a description. Makes one page they keep and grow, with each step, who does it, who checks it, and how to run it in Cowork. ([columbia-university-school-of-the-arts/cms-f26](https://github.com/columbia-university-school-of-the-arts/cms-f26/tree/HEAD/plugins/cms-workflow/skills/workflow-map) · ★3)
- **workflow-map**: DevOKs SDLC 작업의 전체 흐름(단계 순서)과 각 단계의 진입 스킬·커맨드를 한 장의 표로 보여준다. 파이프라인(준비→제품·기술·화면 설계(선택)→요구정의→작업분해→구현→검증체인→커밋/PR)과 순서 없는 단발 작업을 구분해 제시한다. "워크플로우 맵", "SDLC 흐름 보여줘", "스킬 표", "어떤 스킬 써야 해", "다음 단계 뭐야", "작업 순서 알려줘", "스킬 목록 정리해줘" 같은 조회성 요청에서 사용한다. 표를 출력할 뿐 어떤 단계도 실행하지 않는다 — 실제 실행은 표에 적힌 각 스킬을 직접 호출한다. ([ridsync/devoks-team-harness](https://github.com/ridsync/devoks-team-harness/tree/HEAD/plugins/devoks-sdlc/skills/workflow-map) · ★1)
- **workflow-mapper**: Maps business processes into text-based flowcharts with decision points, handoffs, and timing estimates, then identifies bottlenecks and automation opportunities. Use when a user wants to document how their business actually works, find inefficiencies, visualize a process, or decide what to automate.
